Method: 提出TAVR-VLM框架，核心为Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention（R-CGA）。该机制构建“风险→区域→词”的内部结构化 grounding 路径：先将多模态输入压缩为因果风险瓶颈，生成全局风险支持掩码；再在自回归生成过程中，通过support-projected causal consistency目标约束token级 grounding，使生成内容落在风险定义的解剖支持区域内。

Result: 在包含1,482名患者的M³TAVR队列上，模型取得新的最佳性能：AUROC达到0.896，CIDEr达到0.936，幻觉率降低至8.1%。

Conclusion: TAVR-VLM通过显式风险条件化和因果一致性约束提升了医学多模态生成的解剖可解释性与可靠性，为循证外科AI提供了更可信的技术路径。

Abstract: Transcatheter Aortic Valve Replacement (TAVR) planning requires meticulous multimodal reasoning. However, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this high-stakes domain is severely impeded by diagnostic hallucinations, where generated text lacks anatomical grounding. To address this, TAVR-VLM is introduced: a novel framework featuring Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention (R-CGA) that instantiates a model-internal ``Risk $\rightarrow$ Region $\rightarrow$ Word'' structural grounding pathway. R-CGA compresses multimodal inputs into a causal risk bottleneck, purifying dense visual features into a global risk mask. During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>


### [216] [A Pipeline for Generating Longitudinal Synthetic Clinical Notes Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26879)
*William Poulett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个合成临床病历笔记生成流水线与数据集，用于在不依赖真实患者数据的情况下开发和评估临床AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床文档包含高度敏感信息，获取和共享受限；临床AI工具仍需要具有真实感、纵向一致性和多样性的测试数据来支持开发、验证与评估。

Method: 构建模块化生成流程：先生成结构化患者信息，再模拟半结构化住院诊疗旅程，最后使用大语言模型生成非结构化临床笔记；同时加入基于LLM的验证与增强步骤，以提升笔记的忠实性、现实感、多样性和纵向内部一致性。

Result: 发布包含70名合成患者的数据集，每名患者对应20–50份覆盖完整住院过程的临床笔记；数据集提供多个验证级别，便于用户在真实性与规模化之间权衡。

Conclusion: 该数据集可支持临床AI系统的开发、测试和评估，例如摘要生成、医学编码和决策支持，同时降低使用真实患者数据带来的隐私风险。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to enable the development and evaluation of AI systems in domains where access to real-world data is restricted. In healthcare, clinical documentation presents particular challenges due to its sensitivity. This work introduces a synthetic clinical notes pipeline and dataset designed to support the development of clinical AI tools while avoiding the privacy risks associated with real patient data. The dataset is generated using a modular pipeline that combines structured patient generation, semi-structured patient journey simulation, and unstructured clinical note generation using large language models. The pipeline is designed to prioritise internal consistency across longitudinal patient records, while also capturing variation in writing style, note structure, and clinical detail. Additional mechanisms, including LLM-based validation and augmentation steps, are used to improve faithfulness, realism, and diversity of the generated notes. We release a dataset of 70 synthetic patients, each associated with 20-50 clinical notes spanning a full hospital journey. The dataset is provided at multiple levels of validation, enabling users to balance realism and scalability depending on their use case. This dataset supports the development, testing, and evaluation of clinical AI systems, including summarisation tools, coding models, and decision support systems, without reliance on real patient data.

</details>


### [217] [Generative Retrieval via Diffusion Transformer with Metric-Ordered Sequence Training and Hybrid-Policy Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26899)
*Chenghao Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhongtao Jiang,Kun Xu,Zhenwei An,Renzhi Wang,Zhao Wang,Jiachen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhang,Kun Xu,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向“保持细粒度模式且满足目标属性”的嵌入检索任务，并用连续生成式检索框架 MO-DiT+HPPO 显著提升交集指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统嵌入检索通常返回与查询最相似或全局属性得分最高的项目，但生产场景中常需要基于种子集合扩展出既符合目标属性、又保持种子所表达细粒度模式的项目。简单平均种子会保留模式但属性不足，全局属性检索则容易偏离原模式，因此需要兼顾模式纯度与属性提升的新方法。

Method: 作者形式化提出 pattern-preserving attribute retrieval，并采用连续生成式检索：模型读取项目嵌入序列，生成用于近邻搜索的查询嵌入。方法 MO-DiT+HPPO 包括原始序列预训练、多领域按指标排序的继续预训练、尾部质心微调，以及 HPPO。指标排序训练将稀疏在线检索标签转化为从低到高属性密度的模式内轨迹；HPPO 使用混合候选池的在线交集指标标注偏好对，并通过参考锚定偏好优化对齐真实目标，同时用 Pareto pair filter 避免降低同模式纯度。

Result: 在四个属性领域、项目保持和模式保持两类划分协议下，metric-ordered DiT 相比预训练生成式检索器提升交集指标，HPPO 进一步提升；八个领域划分单元中七个取得显著增益，最困难划分上为边际持平。消融实验验证了指标预测器、排序策略、CPT/SFT 对比和候选策略对收益来源的贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将属性提升方向建模为跨领域的有序轨迹，并用在线目标驱动的偏好优化进行对齐，可以在不牺牲细粒度模式一致性的前提下提升目标属性检索效果，适合需要模式保持的生产级嵌入检索场景。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval ranks items by their similarity to a query in a shared vector space and usually aims to return the highest-scoring items. In many production settings this is not what is wanted: given a seed set that expresses a fine-grained pattern, one needs more items that both satisfy a target attribute and stay within that pattern. We formalize this as pattern-preserving attribute retrieval. The two goals pull against each other: averaging the seeds preserves the pattern but stays in a low-attribute region, while global attribute retrieval drifts to unrelated patterns. We approach the task with continuous generative retrieval, where a model reads a sequence of item embeddings and generates query embeddings for nearest-neighbor search. We propose MO-DiT+HPPO, a staged framework with raw-sequence pretraining, multi-domain metric-ordered continuation pretraining, tail-centroid fine-tuning, and HPPO. Metric-ordered training turns sparse online retrieval labels into in-pattern trajectories ordered from low to high predicted attribute density, teaching one model the metric-improvement direction across domains. HPPO aligns the generated query distribution with the true online objective by labeling a hybrid candidate pool with the online intersection metric and applying reference-anchored preference optimization. A Pareto pair filter keeps only winner pairs that do not lower same-pattern purity, raising the attribute metric without sacrificing the pattern. Across four attribute domains under item- and pattern-holdout protocols, metric-ordered DiT improves the intersection metric over a pretrained generative retriever, and HPPO improves it further, with significant gains on seven of eight domain-split cells and a marginal tie on the hardest split. Metric-predictor validation, order ablations, CPT/SFT comparisons, and a candidate-policy ablation show where the gains come from.

</details>


### [218] [Learning to Recover Task Experts from a Multi-Task Merged Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26902)
*Jinwook Jung,Taegyu Kim,Kumju Jo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTeX 从单一合并检查点中预测并补偿任务专家在模型合并中受到的参数扰动，从而恢复专家性能，并在未知任务上实现自适应专家知识插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务模型合并希望将多个任务专用专家整合为一个统一模型，但静态合并容易产生参数干扰；动态合并虽能缓解问题，却通常需要在推理时存储和加载冗余专家组件，带来较高成本。因此需要一种既能保留单一合并模型部署优势、又能恢复任务专家能力的方法。

Method: 论文将合并过程中的参数干扰视为施加在每个任务专家上的参数扰动，并指出该扰动可建模为仿射变换、近似为加性偏移。基于此提出 ReTeX，通过预测参数 offset 来抵消干扰、恢复任务专家性能。对于任务身份未知的情况，方法在推理前离线计算基于 SVD 的任务子空间签名；推理时使用无路由器任务识别器，选择对输入投影残差最小的任务子空间，从而确定应恢复的专家。

Result: ReTeX 在视觉和 NLP 任务中均能恢复超过 95% 的单个专家性能，并显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力。论文还观察到参数 offset 预测会产生对分布外任务的自适应专家知识插值现象。

Conclusion: ReTeX 提供了一种轻量的动态恢复机制，在无需推理时加载多个冗余专家的情况下，从单一合并检查点中缓解参数干扰、恢复专家能力，并通过自适应插值提升 OOD 与未见任务表现。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging aims to consolidate several task-specific experts into a unified model, yet static merging consistently suffers from parameter interference. While dynamic merging models aim to bridge this gap, many works rely on the costly storage and loading of redundant expert components at inference. In this work, from the perspective of task expert, we view parameter interference as parameter perturbation introduced to each expert during merging process. We show that such parameter perturbations can be modeled as affine transformation, which can be approximated as additive offsets. Motivated by these, we propose Recover Task eXpert (ReTeX), a framework that predicts those offsets, in order to undo parameter interference and recover task-expert performance from a single merged checkpoint. To recover the appropriate expert when task identity is unknown, we introduce a router-free task identifier based on SVD subspace signatures computed offline before inference. At inference, the identifier selects the task whose subspace yields the smallest projection residual for a given input. As a result, ReTeX recovers over 95% of individual-expert performance in both vision and NLP domains, while significantly improving generalization to unseen tasks. Crucially, we also show that the parameter offset prediction leads to emergent adaptive interpolation of expert knowledge for out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. ReTeX adaptively interpolates seen expert knowledge to handle unseen tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BAIKLAB/ReTeX

</details>


### [219] [Diagnosing Task Insensitivity in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26918)
*Jingyu Liu,Xiaopeng Wu,Kehan Chen,Chuan Yu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出大型语言模型作为长程智能体时存在任务不敏感问题，并提出一种轻量级对比正则化方法来增强动作对任务指令的依赖，从而提升OOD泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长程任务中虽表现出较强能力，但面对分布外任务时泛化较弱。作者认为关键原因之一是模型会依赖训练中学到的模式或捷径，而不是充分理解当前任务指令。

Method: 作者通过语义破坏指令、替换训练提示中的任务描述等方式诊断任务不敏感现象，并分析训练过程中的注意力漂移。随后提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization，在负对数似然目标中加入轻量级对比正则项，显式鼓励模型输出动作依赖任务指令。

Result: 实验表明，该方法提升了模型对任务描述变化的敏感性，改善了分布外泛化能力，并使注意力更稳定地保持在任务相关token上。

Conclusion: 任务不敏感是长程智能体OOD泛化失败的重要来源，而通过任务扰动式NLL优化可以有效缓解模型对局部观察或训练捷径的过度依赖。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>


### [220] [Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26935)
*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究CoT训练究竟提升了模型的显式推理能力，还是提升了其不依赖CoT、直接从提示中预测动作的能力；结果表明后者贡献显著，并提出部分屏蔽动作token监督以改善域外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出语言模型生成的CoT并不总是忠实反映真实决策过程，可能只是事后解释。因而需要澄清：CoT训练带来的性能提升，是否真正来自生成推理对动作选择的因果影响，还是模型学会了更好地直接从输入提示预测正确动作。

Method: 作者比较两类动作预测：不生成CoT时的prompt actions，以及生成CoT后的CoT actions；在不同训练checkpoint上评估二者质量、交互环境中的相对优势，以及CoT是否会改变模型原本动作。基于观察到的趋势，作者进一步在部分训练样本上选择性屏蔽动作token监督，测试其对泛化的影响。

Result: 随着训练推进，prompt-action质量显著提升；在环境交互中，CoT actions相对prompt actions的优势并未扩大，说明CoT训练并没有增强CoT推理对动作改动的额外作用。后期checkpoint更少因CoT而修改动作，表现出更强的提示依赖。屏蔽部分动作token监督可提升域外泛化。

Conclusion: CoT训练的收益可能主要来自改进直接动作预测，而非增强显式CoT推理的因果作用。减少对动作token的直接监督可缓解模型过度依赖提示到动作的捷径，从而改善域外泛化。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [221] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Look-Before-Move 框架，将“先确定应观察什么”与“再生成相机运动”分离，用于动态 3D 故事世界中的叙事驱动相机规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 3D 环境中的具身 AI 与世界模型需要从被动视觉理解转向主动视觉注意；相机不仅要移动平滑，还要在移动前根据叙事意图和物理约束决定观察对象、构图方式与注意力转移。

Method: 作者将问题定义为 Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention，并提出三阶段框架：构建 Semantic Observation Contract 将导演意图转化为可执行视觉约束；通过 Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search 搜索符合叙事与几何可行性的视点；使用 Semantic Trajectory Grounding 将视点连接为连续、避碰且时间一致的相机轨迹。

Result: 作者基于 StoryBlender 构建动态 3D Story World Benchmark，包含 50 个故事、457 个场景和 1585 个镜头。实验表明该框架在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量上优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: 研究证明，在生成相机运动之前组织视觉注意对于动态 3D 故事世界中的相机规划至关重要，Look-Before-Move 能更好地兼顾叙事语义、几何约束与运动连贯性。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [222] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Einstein World Models（EWM）：让大语言模型在推理过程中调用视觉-时间世界模块，生成短时场景展开，将其作为可检查的假设来辅助后续推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注智能是否需要超越直接经验的反事实想象能力，并质疑仅依赖语言是否足以支持复杂思维；因此探索视觉化反事实事件是否能补充语言推理。

Method: 构建一种 LLM 推理系统蓝图：在推理轨迹中嵌入视觉-时间 rollout。LLM 调用 world-module 生成待考察场景的短序列展开，再将该展开作为中间推理证据或假设，而非最终答案。

Result: 摘要主要提出概念性框架，未报告具体实验结果；其预期效果是扩展 LLM 的工具调用能力，使模型能够进行类似视觉思想实验的推理。

Conclusion: Einstein World Models 旨在突破纯文本推理的局限，通过可检查的视觉-时间模拟增强 LLM 对反事实场景和复杂现象的推理能力。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AURORA-AI：一种面向非平稳部署环境的自适应资源编排框架，通过闭环控制在多模型群体间动态分配计算预算，以同时优化性能、公平性、成本、时延、鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 系统运行环境会发生计算资源、用户分布与业务条件变化，静态资源分配容易导致预测性能下降，并损害公平性、可解释性等以人为中心的属性，因此需要具备稳定性保障和公平性意识的自适应编排方法。

Method: AURORA-AI 将 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman 反馈控制、Lyapunov 稳定性监测和公平性感知复合效用函数整合为单一闭环策略，持续在异构 AI 模型群体间重新分配计算预算。效用函数联合考虑预测性能、人口统计均等性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性，并在包含偏差冲击、概念漂移和黑天鹅扰动的离散时间仿真中评估。

Result: 相较 Static、Round Robin、Greedy、LinUCB 和基于 PPO 的深度强化学习控制器，AURORA-AI 在黑天鹅事件后实现即时恢复；Static 需 88 个时间步，PPO 需 22 个时间步。其 alpha-quantile 和 super-quantile 分别提升 29% 与 25%，同时降低平均和最大人口统计均等性差距，并提高 Lyapunov 稳定运行步数占比。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将稳定性理论与公平性感知效用优化结合的自适应资源编排，是提升 AI 系统在扰动环境下韧性、性能与人本属性的一条可行且具有理论依据的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [224] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究多智能体 LLM 循环中的语义早停，用语义变化与质量改进信号替代固定迭代上限；在 HotpotQA 上，免评审的语义停止器以近似质量持平节省 38% 运行 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定 max_iterations 只是一种语法层面的终止开关，无法判断答案是否仍在语义或质量上改进，导致简单问题浪费 token、困难问题可能被过早截断。

Method: 提出基于连续草稿嵌入余弦距离和 patience 窗口的语义早停，并结合质量改进门控；给出确定性终止与良定义性的理论证明和机器检查；设计一次生成完整轨迹、复放不同停止策略并缓存 LLM judge 调用的成对评估协议，区分运行 token 与评估 token。

Result: 在 HotpotQA 60 题测试集上，judge-free 语义停止器相对 max_iterations 减少 38% 运行 token，质量基本持平（Delta-IS=-0.004, p=0.81）；完整质量门控版本因逐轮评审成本过高而得不偿失；oracle 最优轮选择比所有实用策略高 +0.115 Information Score（p≈4e-11）。

Conclusion: 语义早停能在不牺牲质量的情况下显著降低多智能体 LLM 循环成本，但逐轮质量评审不经济；未来关键问题不只是何时停止，而是如何可靠识别轨迹中的最佳轮次。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [225] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述有真实标签时用于聚类评估的常见外部有效性指标，重点讨论基于集合匹配的度量，并给出指标选择建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类评估在存在真实标签时可借助外部指标完成，但不同指标的粒度、可解释性以及对簇大小的敏感性不同，实践中需要明确的选择依据。

Method: 作者回顾常见外部有效性指标，尤其关注 set-matching-based measures，并从簇级可解释性、点级精细度、归一化性质、簇大小偏置以及样本等权性等角度进行比较。

Result: 推荐 centroid index（CI）作为直观、簇级、结果可解释的指标；若需要更精细的点级评估，可选择 PSI、ACC 或其他集合匹配度量。PSI 提供归一化且不受簇大小偏置影响的分数；若希望所有样本点等权，则 ACC 或其他集合匹配指标较合适。

Conclusion: 外部聚类评价指标应根据评估目标选择：强调簇级解释性时优先 CI；强调点级精细评价时可考虑 PSI；强调样本等权时可采用 ACC 或相关集合匹配指标。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [226] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JERP将经验规则学习与策略优化联合起来，使LLM智能体能更有效利用多步交互经验，在复杂交互任务中提升决策表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步交互环境中，LLM智能体需要利用历史交互经验。现有方法要么将经验以自然语言规则形式外置用于提示，解释性强但容易与不断变化的策略脱节；要么用轨迹和反馈更新模型参数，能改进整体策略但在稀疏奖励下对局部错误纠正不足。

Method: 提出Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents（JERP）。该方法从同一批交互轨迹中同时更新长期经验规则池和策略。决策时检索与任务相关的规则，并与交互历史一起作为条件输入；每轮结束后，利用轨迹优化策略，同时通过比较当前rollout与成功参考轨迹来修正规则池。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，JERP在复杂交互任务中带来稳定的决策性能提升。

Conclusion: JERP通过耦合经验规则更新与策略学习，使规则池与演化中的策略保持一致，并将稳定有效的行为逐步吸收到模型内部，从而更好地支持LLM智能体的长期经验利用。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [227] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAVE 标注协议与 OpenRCA 2.0 基准，用逐步因果传播路径评估 LLM 智能体的根因分析能力，揭示仅看最终根因会掩盖“未扎根诊断”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RCA 数据集通常只标注最终根因，而不标注从根因到观测症状之间的因果传播路径，导致任务可能退化为模式匹配，无法严格评估 LLM 的长上下文理解、多步推理和工具使用能力。

Method: 作者提出 PAVE，一种逐步标注协议，利用故障注入中的已知干预信息，通过从原因到结果的正向验证方式重建因果传播路径；基于该协议构建 OpenRCA 2.0，包含 500 个跨系统 RCA 实例，并带有逐步因果注释。

Result: 在 11 个前沿 LLM 上，平均只有 20.7% 的案例能够精确恢复完整根因集合。放宽评估后，模型在 76.0% 的案例中能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但只有 61.5% 能将该服务与经过验证的因果传播路径连接到观测症状。

Conclusion: 仅基于最终结果的 RCA 评估会隐藏模型缺乏因果路径支撑的失败模式；逐步因果真值对于构建和评估可信的 LLM RCA 智能体是必要的。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [228] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TOPS：一种免训练、模型无关的视觉 token 剪枝模块，从信息论第一性原理出发，以任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性指导 token 选择，在大幅减少视觉 token 的同时保持甚至提升多模态大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型因视觉 token 数量庞大而计算开销高。已有剪枝方法存在局限：基于注意力的方法容易保留冗余 token，基于多样性的方法往往忽视用户指令，即便组合多种准则，也缺少对视觉 token 剪枝内在目标的原则性刻画。

Method: 作者将视觉 token 剪枝重新形式化为构建 Token Optimal Preservation Sets，并通过自顶向下的信息论分析提出三项基本原则：任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性。在此基础上设计 TOPS，一个无需训练且模型无关的剪枝模块，可插入不同 MLLM 骨干模型。

Result: 在 7 个 MLLM 骨干和 14 个基准上的实验显示，TOPS 在多种剪枝设置下优于已有方法。特别是在 LLaVA-NeXT 上，TOPS 移除 77.8% 的视觉 token 后，7B 和 13B 模型分别保持 100.0% 和 100.6% 的性能。

Conclusion: TOPS 表明，基于明确原则的视觉 token 选择能够显著降低多模态推理成本，同时保持性能；去除冗余视觉 token 还可能缓解幻觉问题，并为轻量化 MLLM 设计提供启发。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [229] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 process harness：在不替换既有确定性工作流引擎的前提下，为传统流程外加一个受策略治理的智能体层，以实现 Agentic BPM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工作流引擎擅长保证结构化、确定性的流程执行，但难以处理需要推理、动态适应和运行时监督的知识密集型场景。作者希望在保留既有引擎合规性与稳定性的同时，引入 LLM/智能体能力。

Method: 作者定义 Task-Decision-Flow（TDF）模型，给出数据模式与执行语义；将 LLM 推理拆分为 TaskAgent、DecisionAgent、FlowAgent 三类受策略约束的智能体，并通过 hook 机制在指定控制点介入流程。所有智能体调用均受 process FRAME 中的显式策略集合约束。随后实现 CUGA FLO 作为 TDF 的设计与系统落地。

Result: 论文在贷款审批工作流上展示了该机制，覆盖三类智能体的执行，并演示了由 hook 驱动的运行时流程适应与监管覆盖能力。

Conclusion: process harness 在确定性工作流引擎的结构权威与策略约束下的智能体自治之间建立折中，使传统 BPM 能在不替换核心引擎的情况下获得推理、适应和监督能力。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [230] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GAversary，一种面向 NLP 模型的黑盒遗传算法对抗样本生成方法，仅依赖目标模型输出的 logit 值进行搜索，并通过 GloVe 嵌入指导词替换以提升语义相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习 NLP 模型容易受到对抗输入影响；现有攻击方法常依赖模型内部信息或对脆弱词定位，黑盒场景下仍需在攻击成功率、语义保持和扰动规模之间取得平衡。

Method: GAversary 使用混合遗传算法生成文本对抗样本，将目标模型视为黑盒，以 logit 输出作为适应度信号；在变异操作中利用 GloVe 词向量提出候选替换词，以保持替换词与原词的语义接近。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上，GAversary 相比 BAE 和 A2T 能更显著降低模型测试准确率；最佳情况下将 76.8% 的准确率降至 5.8%，优于 BAE 的 27.6%。代价是扰动词数量接近其他方法的两倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间约增加 5%。

Conclusion: GAversary 在黑盒设定下具有较强的攻击有效性，适合评估 NLP 模型鲁棒性，但其更高的扰动比例和略低语义相似度表明仍存在自然性与攻击强度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [231] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL 将评价标准拆解为原子级二元问题，并聚合判定结果，形成可解释、多维度且校准的生成质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 输出评估存在瓶颈：人工评估成本高、速度慢；词汇匹配指标难以衡量开放式生成；整体式 LLM 评审给出的分数不透明，难以调试与诊断。

Method: 框架根据任务提示用元提示生成细粒度评价问题；LLM 针对每个候选输出独立回答这些二元问题；再将问题级判定聚合为多维度和总体评分，同时提供透明的反馈，用于诊断和提示优化。

Result: 在 SummEval、Topical-Chat 和 QAGS 上，BINEVAL 达到或超过 UniEval、G-Eval 等强基线；在事实一致性任务如 QAGS 上表现尤为突出，并更贴近人类评分分布，减少传统 LLM 评审的高分天花板效应。

Conclusion: BINEVAL 是一种任务无关、无需训练、可解释的 LLM 输出评估框架，不仅具有较强的人类相关性，还能提供诊断反馈并支持迭代式提示优化。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [232] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EO-WM：一种用于多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散 Transformer，将未来地表动态预测建模为受天气驱动且部分可观测的世界建模问题，并重点评估模型对天气变化的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测预测方法存在两类不足：确定性模型无法表达未来不确定性，扩散模型通常将气象变量作为笼统条件处理；同时，现有基准多关注像素重建精度，较少检验预测是否能对变化的天气强迫产生合理响应。

Method: EO-WM 采用视频扩散 Transformer，并引入物理启发的气象条件框架：将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理胁迫信号；通过不同条件路径分别建模基线与异常，并随时间累积异常强迫以刻画持续热胁迫和干旱胁迫。论文还提出两个诊断性评测基准：极端夏季基准和季节匹配对基准。

Result: 实验表明，EO-WM 在预测 NDVI 下降幅度误差上相对降低 5.63%，方向命中率相对提升 7.80%，同时在常规像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将气象条件进行物理结构化建模并引入天气响应诊断基准，有助于提升地球观测预测模型在极端或变化天气条件下的响应真实性与可解释性。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [233] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估用基于神经后验估计的仿真式推断（SBI）替代 MCMC，对机械式 SECIR 流行病学模型进行贝叶斯校准；在 ICU 占用数据任务中，SBI 在保持与 MCMC 相近后验与预测表现的同时显著缩短运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械式流行病学模型常用于疾病预测与公共卫生决策，但传统 MCMC 贝叶斯校准在高维、非线性系统及近实时重复分析中计算代价高，因此需要更快且可靠的推断方法。

Method: 作者使用德国 2020 年 COVID-19 ICU 占用数据，对 SECIR 模型进行校准；比较 SBI（神经后验估计）与 MCMC 在多个疫情阶段中的表现，包括 31 天推断窗口和更复杂的 201 天多传播变化点重构任务；用 Wasserstein 距离、KL 散度和后验预测检验评估一致性。

Result: 在 31 天窗口中，SBI 得到的后验分布与 MCMC 高度一致，并能准确复现 ICU 轨迹；在 201 天任务中，SBI 虽不确定性增加，但保留主要后验结构。SBI 在单 GPU 上约 60–70 秒完成 31 天任务，而 MCMC 约需 1000 秒；201 天任务 SBI 平均 157 秒，MCMC 超过 19000 秒。

Conclusion: SBI 是机械式流行病学模型贝叶斯校准的快速、高效替代方案，适合需要重复、近实时推断和快速疫情分析的场景。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [234] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自动化简历筛选中的提示注入现象，发现其在操纵较少且候选人质量接近时最有效，但随着操纵普及效果迅速失效，并可能引发公平性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于招聘筛选与排序，求职者可能通过不增加真实资质的隐性自我推销文本来策略性影响模型评价，因此需要系统评估这种操纵对排序结果与公平性的影响。

Method: 作者构建受控实验，考察自动化简历筛选场景中的提示注入：比较候选人质量同质与异质条件下，不同注入者比例对LLM排名结果的影响，并分析低质量候选人是否可能借此超过高质量候选人。

Result: 在简历质量同质且少数候选人注入时，提示注入能稳定提高排名；当注入者比例增加时，其优势快速减弱并在广泛使用时崩塌。在质量异质条件下，平均效果较弱，但偶尔会使低质量候选人超过高质量候选人。

Conclusion: LLM简历筛选在操纵行为稀少且候选人质量差异较小时最脆弱；提示注入虽不总是有效，但会带来排序可靠性与公平性问题，提示招聘系统需要更强的鲁棒性与防操纵机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [235] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，多模型LLM系统的准确率提升受“全体模型同时出错率”β限制；常用的平均成对错误相关ρ无法识别该上限。实证显示真实β显著高于高斯copula等模型估计，因此简单增加模型并不必然带来收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由、投票、级联、融合和MoA等多模型系统通常声称可超过单模型性能，但领域内很少报告决定其理论收益上限的关键量：所有候选模型在同一查询上同时失败的概率β。作者希望解释为什么多模型组合在许多可检验任务上增益有限。

Method: 从理论上证明：若策略输出必须是某个成员模型的答案，则其准确率上限为1-β；并说明平均成对错误相关ρ无法唯一决定β。提出用Clopper-Pearson置信界对β进行有限样本估计，从而在训练路由器前评估潜在最大收益。实证部分分析来自21个提供方的67个模型，并比较单因子四分相关校准模型、高斯copula估计与观测到的全错尾部。

Result: 在开放式数学任务中，观测β为0.052，而67模型高斯copula估计为0.023，低估约2.5倍，90%置信区间为1.7到3.4，k=17。在执行评分代码任务中β为0.079。GPQA-Diamond从选择题改为自由回答后β升至0.127，五评审一致性κ为0.73到0.92。低ρ异构集成在同等质量下优于高ρ Self-MoA，但若缺乏强查询级路由信号，组合模型很少超过最佳单模型。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的收益主要来自模型在不同问题上失败，而不是单纯增加模型数量。评估和设计多模型LLM系统时，应报告并控制全体同时失败率β，而不能仅依赖成对错误相关ρ；否则会高估路由、投票或级联等方法的潜在增益。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [236] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语言的数字孪生框架，用大语言模型模拟老年人的对话行为，并通过多头条件变分自编码器评估其身份保真度与认知一致性，用于轻度认知障碍的连续监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化医疗中的数字孪生可用于建模个体健康轨迹；在认知健康场景中，轻度认知障碍早筛仍具挑战，而语言与对话模式可作为非侵入式生物标志物，因此需要一种能够模拟个体语言行为并支持认知状态评估的方法。

Method: 作者构建语言驱动的数字孪生框架，利用大语言模型结合文体特征与上下文元数据生成/模拟老年个体的对话行为；同时提出多头条件变分自编码器，联合评估重构质量并预测认知评分，以衡量生成内容的保真度和认知一致性。

Result: 在 I-CONECT 数据集上的实验表明，该数字孪生能够保留个体特异性的语言特征；其重构误差和 MoCA 预测误差与真实数据相当，并优于基线 GPT 生成响应。

Conclusion: 语言型数字孪生有潜力成为一种可扩展、非侵入式的个性化认知健康监测方案，尤其适用于连续跟踪与早期识别轻度认知障碍风险。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Deletion-Correcting Codes for the $\ell$-Symbol Read Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26434)
*Zuo Ye,Gennian Ge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 ℓ-symbol read channel 中固定数量 ℓ-mer 删除的纠删码，给出删除效应的结构刻画，并基于 check patterns 与 power-sum syndromes 构造多类低冗余删除纠正码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ-symbol read channel 的无噪输出由传输序列所有连续 ℓ-mers 构成，契合纳米孔测序、带连续读头的赛道存储以及序列标注等重叠读取机制；实际中读向量可能发生若干 ℓ-mer 缺失，因此需要在对抗删除模型下设计高效纠删码。

Method: 作者首先刻画删除 ℓ-mers 后为恢复一致性所需插入的结构，证明恢复序列可视为在原序列某些周期子串中删除符号；当 t≤ℓ−2 时，这些删除对应完整最小周期。随后引入 check patterns，并利用 power-sum syndromes 构造 ℓ-read 删除纠正码，针对一般参数与若干特殊参数分别优化构造。

Result: 对任意 ℓ≥2，得到单删除纠正码，冗余为 log⌊(n+2ℓ)/(ℓ−1)⌋。当 2≤t≤ℓ/2 时，构造 q 元 ℓ-read t 删除纠正码，冗余为 t log n+O(1)。当 ℓ=2t−1 且 t≥3 时，构造冗余为 (2t−1)log n+O(1) 的码。对 (ℓ,t)∈{(2,2),(3,2),(3,3)}，进一步给出改进构造，包括二元 ℓ-read 2 删除纠正码冗余 2log n+O(1)，非二元 3-read 2 删除纠正码冗余 3log n+O(1)，二元 3-read 3 删除纠正码冗余 5log n+O(1)，非二元 3-read 3 删除纠正码冗余 7log n+O(1)。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 ℓ-read 删除错误与原序列周期结构之间的联系，并据此系统性地给出多种参数范围下的低冗余删除纠正码，扩展了重叠读通道中删除纠错编码的理论基础与构造方法。

Abstract: This paper studies deletion-correcting codes for the $\ell$-symbol read channel, whose noiseless output is the vector of all consecutive $\ell$-mers of a transmitted sequence. This model is motivated by overlapping-read mechanisms arising in nanopore sequencing, racetrack memories with consecutive read heads, and related sequence-labeling problems. We consider an adversarial setting in which a fixed number of $\ell$-mers are deleted from the read vector. Our first contribution is a structural characterization of the effect of such deletions: after a minimum number of $\ell$-mers are inserted to restore consistency, the resulting sequence is obtained from the transmitted sequence by deleting symbols from certain periodic substrings; when $t\le \ell-2$, these deletions correspond to complete minimum periods. Based on this characterization, we introduce check patterns and construct $\ell$-read deletion-correcting codes via power-sum syndromes. For every $\ell\ge2$, we obtain single-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $\log\lfloor (n+2\ell)/(\ell-1)\rfloor$. For $2\le t\le \ell/2$, we construct $q$-ary $\ell$-read $t$-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $t\log n+O(1)$, and for $\ell=2t-1$ with $t\ge3$, we construct codes with redundancy $(2t-1)\log n+O(1)$. We also study the sporadic parameter pairs $(\ell,t)\in\{(2,2),(3,2),(3,3)\}$ and obtain improved constructions, including binary $\ell$-read $2$-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $2\log n+O(1)$ for $\ell=2,3$, a non-binary $3$-read $2$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $3\log n+O(1)$, a binary $3$-read $3$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $5\log n+O(1)$, and a non-binary $3$-read $3$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $7\log n+O(1)$.

</details>


### [238] [A Low-PAPR, Synchronization-Robust Non-Coherent Grassmannian Modulation for Optical Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26464)
*Eylon E. Krause*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向非相干Grassmannian（酉空时）信号的低PAPR常模星座与相位盲子空间定时恢复方法，在无需载波相位/偏振恢复和导频的情况下兼顾多分支分集、低峰均比和可靠定时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grassmannian星座研究通常假设线性无失真信道/发射机且符号定时已获得，未充分处理实际系统中的发射端PAPR/非线性约束以及非相干接收前的定时恢复问题。

Method: 作者在现有Grassmannian packing基础上加入常模约束，分析PAPR与弦距之间的权衡；同时利用GLRT投影能量对未知载波相位的不变性，推导相位盲子空间定时误差检测器，并设计前馈捕获度量实现无需预先载波或偏振恢复的时钟同步。

Result: 常模设计将0.1% PAPR从6.1 dB降至3.6 dB，低于16-QAM的5.2 dB，但高SNR编码增益约损失1.8 dB；TED在滚降系数低至0.1时仍具有清晰S曲线和稳定锁定点；在块衰落下，估计器SER接近理想定时基准并恢复完整分集，未校正0.35符号定时偏移会使误码率在约0.4处出现平台。

Conclusion: 该方案弥补了非相干Grassmannian信号在低PAPR发射与定时恢复方面的不足，实现了低PAPR、多分支分集和免相位恢复接收的结合；但当前评估基于符号率块衰落抽象，完整光纤、调制器和相位噪声建模仍需后续研究。

Abstract: Non-coherent Grassmannian (unitary space-time) signaling detects on the received subspace, which is invariant to a branch-side (polarization or mode-coupling) rotation and to a phase that is constant over the coherence block. It therefore needs no carrier-phase or polarization recovery within the block and is robust to phase noise when the per-block phase drift is small, while a multi-branch (polarization or spatial) front end harvests diversity without channel estimation or pilots. However, the Grassmannian-constellation literature usually assumes a distortion-free, linear channel and transmitter and already-acquired symbol timing. This paper closes both gaps while reusing off-the-shelf Grassmannian packings. First, we impose a constant-modulus (low peak-to-average-power-ratio, PAPR) constraint on the constellation and quantify the PAPR/chordal-distance trade-off: a constant-modulus design lowers the 0.1% PAPR from 6.1 dB (unconstrained) to 3.6 dB -- 1.6 dB below 16-QAM (5.2 dB) -- easing the optical modulator linear range and the fiber Kerr-nonlinearity penalty, at a ~1.8 dB cost in high-SNR coding gain. Second, we derive a phase-blind subspace timing-error detector (TED) that exploits the invariance of the GLRT projection energy to the unknown carrier phase, plus a feedforward acquisition metric, supplying clock recovery without prior carrier or polarization recovery. The TED yields a clean S-curve with a stable lock point for roll-offs down to beta=0.1. Under block fading the proposed estimator attains genie-timing SER within a fraction of a dB and recovers full diversity, whereas an uncorrected 0.35-symbol timing offset floors the error rate near 0.4. Results use a symbol-rate block-fading abstraction; full fiber, modulator, and phase-noise modeling is future work. The scheme combines low PAPR with the diversity and phase-recovery-free operation of non-coherent reception.

</details>


### [239] [Measured-Pattern-Aware Pinching-Antenna Systems With Coupling-Efficiency Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26471)
*Hao Feng,Hui Yang,Ming Zeng,Yulei Wang,Ebrahim Bedeer,Nian Xia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑实测辐射方向图的 pinching-antenna（PA）系统建模与优化框架，用于更准确地进行PA位置与耦合效率设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PA系统分析常假设各向同性辐射并简化耦合效率，可能忽略PA几何相关方向图以及波导中功率被各PA顺序提取这两个实际因素，从而影响性能评估和设计准确性。

Method: 作者构建了包含外部实测辐射方向图、波导衰减和耦合相关功率提取的PA框架；分别研究单PA的放置规则、多PA相位匹配放置，以及均匀耦合和独立可控耦合下的耦合效率优化。

Result: 单PA场景得到兼顾方向增益、波导损耗和自由空间路径损耗的放置准则，并给出优于固定各向同性天线的耦合效率阈值；多PA均匀耦合场景得到一维最优性条件，发现参与相干合并的相位匹配PA越多，优选耦合效率越低；独立可控耦合场景得到闭式功率分配结构，即有效方向信道更强的PA获得更大的辐射功率份额。

Conclusion: 数值结果表明，在PA系统设计中联合考虑实测辐射方向图感知的位置选择和耦合效率优化十分重要，可显著提升模型真实性与系统性能。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna (PA) systems have been widely investigated as a flexible architecture for waveguide-enabled wireless transmission. Existing analytical models, however, often rely on isotropic radiation assumptions and simplified couplingefficiency settings, which may overlook two practical design factors: the geometry-dependent radiation pattern of each PA and the sequential extraction of guided power along the waveguide. In this paper, we propose a measured-radiation-pattern-aware PA framework that incorporates an externally obtained radiation pattern, waveguide attenuation, and coupling-dependent power extraction. For a single PA, the resulting placement rule balances directional gain, waveguide loss, and free-space path loss, leading to a coupling-efficiency threshold for outperforming a fixed isotropic antenna. For multiple PAs, we study phase-matched placement and coupling-efficiency design under both uniform and independently controllable coupling. The uniform-coupling case yields a one-dimensional optimality condition and reveals that the preferred coupling efficiency decreases as more phasematched PAs participate in coherent combining. The independently controllable case admits a closed-form power-allocation structure, where stronger effective directional channels receive larger radiated power fractions. Numerical results based on a representative measured PA radiation pattern demonstrate the importance of jointly accounting for measured-radiation-patternaware placement and coupling-efficiency optimization.

</details>


### [240] [Tight Lower Bounds and Optimal Constructions of Locally Repairable Convertible Codes in the Split Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26742)
*Haoming Shi,Weijun Fang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究系统型最优距离局部可修复码（LRC）之间的码转换，给出全局分裂场景下稳定最优距离LRCC的读带宽下界，并构造达到下界的方案，从而刻画了 g^I,g^F≤r 范围内的最优读带宽成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式存储系统随规模、可靠性需求和磁盘失效率变化，需要调整纠删码参数；码转换可在不同编码参数间迁移数据，但关键挑战是降低转换过程中的读带宽，同时保持LRC的最优距离和局部修复性质。

Method: 聚焦全局分裂 regime 以及 g^I,g^F≤r 的参数范围，采用信息论方法推导稳定最优距离LRCC的读带宽下界，不假设初始码、最终码或转换过程的线性性；随后基于具有指定修复性质的MDS阵列码构造LRCC，并按 g^F=g^I、g^F>g^I、g^F<g^I 三种情况分别设计。

Result: 所提出的三类构造分别在对应参数关系下达到所证明的读带宽下界，因此在整个 g^I,g^F≤r 范围内实现最优读带宽。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了系统型最优距离LRC在全局分裂场景下稳定码转换的最优读带宽成本，且结论具有较强一般性，因为下界不依赖线性假设。

Abstract: Erasure codes are a key technique for achieving fault-tolerant storage in modern distributed storage systems. As storage systems evolve, their code parameters often need to be adjusted to accommodate changes in storage scale, reliability requirements, and disk failure rates. Such adaptation is performed through code conversion, where data encoded under an initial code are transformed into data encoded under a final code. Convertible codes are designed to carry out this transformation efficiently while preserving desirable properties of the underlying codes.
  In this work, we study conversions between systematic optimal-distance locally repairable codes (LRCs), focusing on the read-bandwidth cost in the global split regime. We concentrate on the parameter range \(g^I,g^F\le r\), where the numbers of initial and final global parity nodes do not exceed the local dimension. For this entire range, we derive read-bandwidth lower bounds for stable optimal-distance locally repairable convertible codes (LRCCs) via an information-theoretic approach, without imposing any linearity assumption on the initial and final codes or on the conversion procedure. We then develop constructions based on MDS array codes with prescribed repair properties. According to the relative sizes of \(g^I\) and \(g^F\), the construction is divided into the three cases \(g^F=g^I\), \(g^F>g^I\), and \(g^F<g^I\); in each case, it attains the corresponding lower bound. Hence we characterize the optimal read-bandwidth cost for stable optimal-distance LRCCs throughout the parameter range \(g^I,g^F\le r\).

</details>


### [241] [Grouped Reverse Importance Sampling for the Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26748)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出分组反向重要性采样（GRIS）来估计配分函数，通过将样本按组联合加权以降低均方误差；理论上证明有效改进必须依赖组内耦合，并且非重叠分组下可将最优权重搜索限制为仅依赖总能量的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通反向重要性采样（RIS）对每个样本独立加权，在估计 Boltzmann 分布配分函数时可能具有较高均方误差。作者希望通过样本分组与联合权重设计，寻找可计算且误差更小的估计方法。

Method: 提出多种 GRIS 变体：非重叠分组（NOL）、固定权重滑动窗口（FSW）和可变权重滑动窗口（VSW）。利用归一化 MSE 与联合权重分布和 k 重独立能量和分布之间的卡方散度的恒等关系进行理论分析，并研究权重函数结构，特别是组能量权重函数。

Result: 理论结果表明，任何优于普通 RIS 的权重必须耦合组内样本，乘积分解形式的权重会使 MSE 变差；对于非重叠分组，在不损失最优性的情况下可只考虑依赖组总能量的权重。数值上，k=2 和 k=3 的 NOL 在三个例子中将 MSE 降低约 20%–65%；FSW 优于 NOL，VSW 进一步提升。

Conclusion: GRIS 通过组内联合加权显著改善配分函数估计效率，且组能量权重为理论与实践提供了简化路径；滑动窗口方案尤其是 VSW 展现出更强的数值性能，但滑动窗口下对应的最优性理论仍有开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze several grouped variants of the method of reverse importance sampling (RIS) for estimating a partition function from samples of the Boltzmann distribution $p(x)=e^{ \betaU(x)}/Z(β)$. Ordinary RIS weighs each sample separately. By contrast, our proposed grouped RIS (GRIS) methods are based on assigning the samples into groups (or batches) of size $k\ge 2$ and applying a joint weight function to each group. The focal point of the research is the quest for a tractable weight function that would yield the smallest possible mean squared error (MSE). A simple identity relates the normalized MSE to the chi-squared divergence between the joint-weight distribution and the distribution of the $k$-fold sum of independent energies. Our first theoretical finding is that any weight that improves on ordinary RIS ($k=1$) must couple the group components. In other words, it must not be a product-form function across those components, as product-form weight functions always worsen the MSE. Our second, and more important, finding is that, without loss of optimality, it is sufficient to seek weight functions that depend only on the total energy, $\sum_iU(x_i)$, of the group (group-energy weight functions); for the sliding-window variants, the analogous result is open. This finding simplifies both the theoretical analysis and the application of the method substantially. For $k=2$ and $k=3$, the MSE associated with non-overlapping (NOL) groups is reduced by $20$--$65\%$ across three examples. We then propose two additional variants of GRIS, both based on sliding-window grouping (as opposed to NOL grouping). The first applies a fixed weight sliding window (FSW) across all (cyclic) shifts of the sliding window, and the second allows a variable-weight sliding window (VSW). The FSW scheme improves on the NOL one, and the VSW improves even further, as will be demonstrated numerically.

</details>


### [242] [An Orthogonal Approximate Message Passing Framework for Multiuser Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26777)
*Burak Çakmak,Hao Yan,Alexander Fengler,Giuseppe Caire,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种适用于随机预编码线性高斯多用户通信系统的贝叶斯最优迭代信号恢复算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放问题是：在具有随机预编码、一般线性信道以及非可分编码先验的多用户通信系统中，如何构造可达到贝叶斯最优性能的迭代恢复算法，并给出严格的高维有限样本分析。

Method: 作者建立接收模型 y = Σ_u H_u Ξ_u s_u + n，其中预编码矩阵来自右酉不变随机矩阵系综，用户信号服从一般非因子化分布。提出一种新的 OAMP/VAMP 类型框架，可理解为 Minka 的期望传播 EP 与 OAMP 之间的插值，并对算法进行高维有限样本分析；同时利用新的 disorder-averaging 技术和 replica-symmetric ansatz 分析相应推断问题。

Result: 严格的高维算法分析与 RS ansatz 推导出一致的解耦原理，表明复杂多用户随机预编码系统在渐近极限下可等效分解为更简单的标量/有效信道推断问题。

Conclusion: 在 RS ansatz 成立的条件下，所提出的 OAMP/VAMP 型算法渐近达到贝叶斯最优性能，解决了该类线性高斯多用户通信系统中贝叶斯最优迭代信号恢复算法构造的开放问题。

Abstract: We solve the open problem of constructing a Bayes-optimal iterative signal recovery algorithm for linear-Gaussian \emph{multiuser} communication systems with random precoding at the transmitters. Specifically, we consider the received signal model $\mathbf{y} = \sum_{u} \mathbf{H}_u \mathbfΞ_u \mathbf{s}_u + \mathbf{n}$, where $\mathbf{n}$ is white Gaussian noise, $\{\mathbf{H}_u \in \mathbb{C}^{L \times L}\}$ are discrete-time channel matrices -- modeling a wide class of generally time-varying and dispersive linear channels with possibly multiple antennas -- and the precoding matrices $\{\boldsymbolΞ_u \in \mathbb{C}^{L \times N_u}\}$ are drawn independently from a right-unitarily invariant random matrix ensemble. We consider generic \emph{non-separable} (coded) systems where the users' signals $\{\mathbf{s}_u\}$ follow general (non-factorizing) distributions. For this model, we introduce a novel orthogonal/vector approximate message passing (OAMP/VAMP)-type framework, including an algorithm and its high-dimensional (but finite-sample) analysis. From an algorithmic standpoint, the proposed method can be interpreted as an \emph{interpolation} between Minka's expectation propagation (EP)--a widely used method in machine learning--and OAMP. Our main theoretical contribution is the explicit finite-sample analysis of the proposed algorithm. Furthermore, we analyze the associated inference problem via a replica-symmetric (RS) ansatz by using a novel disorder-averaging technique. Both the (rigorous) high-dimensional analysis of the algorithm and the RS ansatz reveal the same decoupling principle, establishing that the proposed algorithm is asymptotically Bayes-optimal under the validity of the RS ansatz.

</details>


### [243] [An Information-Theoretic Metric for Semantic Value of Spatiotemporal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26844)
*Zijing Wang,Zhijin Qin,Siyu Lin,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息的语义信息价值（SVoI）度量，用于量化时空相关语义信息对未知系统状态预测的不确定性降低，并在高斯马尔可夫模型下给出闭式表达与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络规模与数据量快速增长导致频谱资源受限，语义通信希望通过传输面向任务的关键信息缓解带宽瓶颈；但实际实时应用中，信息语义价值受源动态、环境动态、时空相关性、信息时效性和信道条件共同影响，亟需统一的信息论度量。

Method: 构建基于互信息的SVoI框架，将语义价值定义为利用过去时空相关观测预测未知系统状态时所带来的不确定性降低；针对一般高斯马尔可夫模型推导SVoI闭式表达，并分析可分离与耦合时空相关结构对SVoI的影响，同时通过数值仿真验证理论结果及其界。

Result: 理论上获得了高斯马尔可夫场景下SVoI的闭式公式，并刻画了不同空间、时间及耦合相关性对语义价值的影响；仿真结果验证了SVoI理论分析和边界的正确性。

Conclusion: SVoI能够联合表征源的语义时空相关性、信息时效性与信道条件，可作为下一代语义感知无线通信系统设计与优化的有效目标函数。

Abstract: With the explosive growth of network scale and data volume, wireless communication is facing an increasingly severe limitation of spectrum resources. Semantic communication has emerged as a promising paradigm to break the bandwidth bottleneck by transmitting significant task-oriented semantic information rather than raw data. In practical real-time wireless applications, semantics of information exhibit diverse spatial and temporal correlations depending on intrinsic dynamics of source and extrinsic dynamics of environment. Motivated by this observation, this paper develops a novel information-theoretic metric to quantify the semantic value of spatiotemporal information. Specifically, a semantic value of information (SVoI) framework is proposed based on the mutual information, which characterises the reduction in uncertainty when predicting an unknown system state using past semantic spatiotemporal correlated observations. Focusing on general Gaussian Markov models, closed-form expressions of the SVoI are derived. Effects of both separable and coupled spatiotemporal correlations on SVoI are further investigated analytically. Numerical simulations are conducted to validate the theoretical analysis of SVoI and its bounds. The proposed SVoI metric jointly captures the impact of semantic spatiotemporal correlation of source, timeliness of information, and channel conditions, which could serve as an effective optimisation objective for the design of next-generation semantic-aware communication systems.

</details>


### [244] [A $q$-ary Local Criterion for the Radius-One Limited Permutation Channel and Almost-Optimal Binary Block-Concatenation Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26905)
*Noam Ben Shimon,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究半径为1的有限置换信道的零错误块拼接码，提出局部判定准则、验证器与检测码构造，并在二进制情形改进已知码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局零错误混淆判定通常涉及所有长度的码字组合，复杂且难以构造高码率码；同时反馈场景下也需要研究错误检测能力。

Method: 提出q元两阶段局部准则：同长度截断球测试与不同长度前缀测试；证明该充分准则在码率意义上完备；构造精确乘积自动机验证器；并给出q元错误检测的配对上界和局部检测准则。

Result: 二进制纠错块拼接码达到0.649872、0.652018、0.653618的码率，优于先前0.642805，距2/3上界仅0.013049；二进制检测码率达到0.756707，上界为1/2 log2 3≈0.792481。

Conclusion: 局部准则可将全局混淆问题化为有限局部检查，且无渐近码率损失；所给构造推进了LPC∞(1)零错误纠错与检测码的已知性能。

Abstract: The radius-one limited permutation channel $\operatorname{LPC}_{\infty}(1)$ maps a transmitted word to any word obtained by an arbitrary set of pairwise disjoint adjacent transpositions. This is the $r=1$ case of the $\ell_\infty$-limited permutation channel of Langberg et al., and is also the zero-error version of simultaneous adjacent-swap errors. We study zero-error block-concatenation codes for this channel.
  Our first contribution is a $q$-ary two-stage local criterion for certifying free block-concatenation codes. The criterion replaces the global all-length confusability problem by finitely many local checks between blocks: a same-length truncated-ball test and a second-stage prefix test for unequal lengths. In the binary case, it yields explicit block-concatenation codes of rates $0.649872$, $0.652018$, and $0.653618$. The best construction improves the previous string-concatenation rate $0.642805$ and comes within $0.013049$ of the known upper bound $2/3$.
  Although the criterion is only sufficient, we prove that it is rate-complete: for every alphabet size $q$, the supremum of $q$-ary block rates certified by the criterion is exactly the $q$-ary zero-error capacity $C_0^{(q)}$. Thus it imposes no asymptotic rate loss. We also give an exact product-automaton verifier which decides, for a fixed prefix-free binary block set, whether the induced finite-length codes are correcting for all lengths.
  Finally, motivated by feedback settings, we study error detection. We prove a $q$-ary pairing upper bound and give a $q$-ary local detecting criterion. In the binary case, we construct a detecting block-concatenation code of rate $0.756707$, compared with the upper bound $\frac12\log_2 3\approx0.792481$.

</details>


### [245] [Mismatched Exponents for Deterministic and Randomised Noise-Guessing Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26954)
*Henrique K. Miyamoto,Richard Combes,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究加性无记忆信道中噪声猜测译码的确定性与随机化版本，在失配度量下分析错误指数与复杂度指数，并比较匹配、α-倾斜和通用译码度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声猜测译码的性能不仅取决于错误概率，也取决于译码复杂度；在实际中译码度量可能与真实信道不匹配，因此需要理解不同度量对错误指数和复杂度指数的影响，并寻找无需信道先验的通用方案。

Method: 作者建立失配译码度量下确定性与随机化噪声猜测译码的指数分析框架，并将结果特化到匹配度量、α-倾斜度量以及基于噪声序列经验熵的通用度量。

Result: 确定性译码中，α-倾斜不会改变性能，所有α>0的倾斜度量与匹配度量等价，并对平均错误和复杂度均最优。随机化译码中，匹配度量对复杂度指数并非最优；需要随码率调节α才能同时达到最优错误指数与复杂度指数。经验熵通用度量可在未知信道律且所有码率上同时达到两类最优指数。

Conclusion: 论文表明确定性与随机化噪声猜测译码在复杂度最优性上存在本质差异；α-倾斜参数在随机化译码中是关键调节量，而经验熵通用度量提供了更稳健、无需信道知识的最优译码方案。

Abstract: We study both the deterministic and randomised variants of noise-guessing decoding in additive memoryless channels. The error and complexity exponents of such decoding schemes are analysed under mismatched decoding metrics, and then specialised to matched, $α$-tilted, and universal decoding metrics. The $α$-tilted metric is proportional to the $α$-th power ($α>0$) of the true noise distribution. In deterministic decoding, the tilting operation does not affect the performance: all these metrics are equivalent to the matched one ($α=1$), and are optimal for both average error and complexity. On the other hand, in randomised decoding, the matched metric is not optimal for complexity exponents; we show that the decoder needs to tune the parameter~$α$ according to the code rate in order to simultaneously achieve both optimal exponents using a decoding metric in that family. Finally, a universal decoding metric based on the empirical entropy of the noise sequence achieves both optimal exponents, independently of the channel law and uniformly over code rates, for the deterministic and randomised variants.

</details>


### [246] [Algorithms for Threshold Group Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27127)
*Amin Coja-Oghlan,Remco van der Hofstad,Lena Krieg,Noela Müller,Connor Riddlesden,Olga Scheftelowitsch*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无噪声、非自适应、无间隔阈值组测试问题，提出一种基于空间耦合测试设计的高效推断算法，在常列设计基准所需的最少测试数下以高概率实现精确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值组测试旨在用尽可能少的池化测试精确恢复稀疏二进制向量。已有结果表明，在解析条件假设下，常列测试设计存在尖锐的信息论相变；但仍需要高效算法达到该信息论阈值，并简化相关组测试算法中复杂的分析。

Method: 作者设计了空间耦合的非自适应测试方案，并配套提出高效推断算法。该算法避免依赖传统二元组测试中复杂加权和的分析，使用更直接的证明框架来建立恢复保证。

Result: 算法在常列设计所需的最小非自适应测试数量下，能够以高概率实现精确恢复，匹配自然基准测试设计的信息论阈值，并具有近最优性能保证。

Conclusion: 空间耦合设计为无间隔阈值组测试提供了算法上高效、理论上近最优且分析更简洁的解决方案，缩小了信息论极限与可计算推断之间的差距。

Abstract: We study the Threshold Group Testing (TGT) problem without a gap in the noiseless, non-adaptive setting, where the goal is to exactly recover a sparse binary vector from pooled test outcomes using as few tests as possible. In TGT, a test applied to a subset of items returns a positive outcome if the number of defective items in the subset reaches a prescribed threshold, and a negative outcome otherwise. Under the assumption of an analytic condition, TGT has been shown to undergo a sharp information-theoretic phase transition for exact recovery on the class of constant-column test designs. In this paper, we develop an efficient inference algorithm that achieves exact recovery with high probability using the minimum number of non-adaptive tests that are needed for the constant-column design, thereby matching the information-theoretic threshold of a natural benchmark test design. Our approach is based on a spatially coupled test design and admits a significantly simpler analysis than existing algorithms for related group testing problems. In particular, unlike previous methods for binary group testing, our algorithm does not rely on the analysis of intricate weighted sums. This leads to a more straightforward proof technique, while still allowing near-optimal performance guarantees.

</details>


### [247] [Linear Code Conversion in the Merge Regime: General Bounds and Reed--Muller Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27179)
*Anina Gruica,Benjamin Jany,Stanislav Kruglik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意标量线性码在合并场景下的码转换成本，提出基于广义汉明重量的通用读写成本下界，并在 Reed-Muller 码上给出可达到部分下界的显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式存储依赖纠删码保证节点失效下的可靠性。随着节点失效率变化，动态调整编码参数可节省存储，但直接重编码会消耗大量系统资源；已有码转换研究多局限于特定码类，缺乏面向任意线性码的统一理论。

Method: 论文研究合并模式下任意线性码的标量线性码转换，围绕未改变符号和读取符号推导写成本与读成本的通用下界；进一步引入广义汉明重量刻画子码支撑集增长性质，从而细化仅依赖最小距离的下界分析；并利用 Plotkin 分解构造 Reed-Muller 可转换码。

Result: 所得框架能够恢复若干重要特例中的已知界；当最终码的广义汉明重量层级存在非平凡跳跃时，新界可严格强于传统最小距离界。对于自然的 Reed-Muller 参数区间，所构造方案达到写成本下界；读成本方面，对其中一个初始块的广义汉明重量分析是紧的，但另一个块仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意线性码合并转换提供了统一的读写成本下界理论，并展示广义汉明重量在刻画码转换复杂度中的优势；Reed-Muller 构造说明该理论具有可实现性，但读成本最优性仍有进一步改进空间。

Abstract: Erasure codes are a core component of most existing large-scale distributed storage systems, ensuring reliability against node failures. Recent work has shown that adapting code parameters to changing node failure rates can lead to significant storage savings. The default approach is to re-encode the data under a new code, which consumes substantial system resources. Code conversion was introduced to reduce this cost. However, existing work has mainly focused on conversions within specific classes of codes. In this paper, we study scalar linear code conversion in the merge regime for arbitrary linear codes. We derive universal lower bounds on the write and read costs in terms of unchanged and read symbols. The bounds are refined using generalized Hamming weights, which capture support-growth properties of subcodes and can give sharper estimates than minimum-distance-only arguments. We show that the framework recovers known bounds for important special cases and can be strictly stronger when the final code has nontrivial jumps in its generalized Hamming weight hierarchy. We then apply the framework to Reed-Muller codes and construct explicit Reed-Muller convertible codes using the Plotkin decomposition. For a natural Reed-Muller parameter regime, the construction attains the derived write-cost lower bound. For the read cost, the generalized-Hamming-weight analysis is sharp for one initial block, while a gap remains for the other block.

</details>


### [248] [All you need is log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27349)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出并刻画了多分布场景下 Rényi 散度的规范推广：所有满足数据处理单调性与独立乘积可加性的 W 元分布泛函，都可表示为多路 coincidence divergences 的正积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Rényi 散度完整解决了两个概率分布之间比较的公理化刻画，但在同时比较多个分布的任务中，如多群体公平性、多先验 PAC-Bayes、多假设检验，缺少统一且公理化的多分布 Rényi 类。

Method: 作者从结构公理出发，研究对 W 元分布满足数据处理不增与独立乘积可加的泛函，并证明其可分解为多路 coincidence divergences C_α 的正积分；同时分析参数空间的四类分层，并通过多种独立推导路径验证该族的自然性。

Result: 得到包含单纯形内部、混合符号指数锥、热带无穷边界上的 max-divergences、以及单纯形顶点处成对 KL 边的完整表示；证明每一层都是必要的，且均可由内部原子极限得到。二分布情形退化为标准 Rényi 结果，并给出 W=3 示例、数值验证和条件扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作为多分布比较建立了统一的 Rényi 型散度演算，表明 multi-way coincidence divergences 是满足核心结构公理的规范多分布推广，而非某一特定公理体系下的偶然产物。

Abstract: Comparing two probability distributions is a basic building block of statistics and machine learning, and the right family is well understood: the Rényi divergences of order $α\in[0,\infty]$ are the unique family monotone under data processing and additive on independent products. Many problems instead compare more than two distributions at once -- multi-population fairness, multi-prior PAC-Bayes bounds, multi-hypothesis testing -- and the right multi-distribution generalization of the Rényi family has been an open question.
  We characterize it. Every functional of $W$-tuples of distributions that is monotone under data processing and additive on independent products is a positive integral of multi-way coincidence divergences $C_α(π_1,\dots,π_W) := -\log\int π_1^{α_1}\cdotsπ_W^{α_W}$ (with $\sum_k α_k = 1$) over a parameter space with four strata: the simplex interior; mixed-sign exponent cones (the analogue of Rényi orders $>1$); a tropical boundary at infinity carrying max-divergences; and pairwise Kullback-Leibler edges at the simplex vertices. Each stratum is necessary -- the destination of an explicit data-processing-monotone, product-additive divergence the others cannot reproduce -- and each is a clean limit of simplex-interior atoms.
  The same family arises from five independent routes -- the structural axioms, Kolmogorov-Nagumo means with Rényi's entropy axiomatics, classical entropy characterizations, multi-hypothesis testing error exponents, and a multi-lottery betting interpretation -- structural evidence that this is the canonical multi-distribution Rényi calculus rather than an artefact of any one axiomatic input. The two-prior case recovers the standard Rényi result; a worked $W=3$ instance, numerical verification, and a conditional extension round out the treatment.

</details>
